# Corpus survey: every EnergyPlus example file

Generated by `python case_studies/corpus_survey.py` (stages `topology`, `scenes`, `report`) on the 719 `ExampleFiles/*.idf` of the EnergyPlus installation thermoscene runs with. Nothing is configured per model: the same code, defaults and automatic choices (`thermoscene/auto.py`) apply to every file. Raw results: `case_studies/corpus_survey.json` (topology), `case_studies/corpus_scenes.json` (scenes).

## Stage 1: topology (no simulation)

**719 example files; 714 loaded and traced (685 of 685 in scope); 4724 zones, 4088 conditioned; 4088 of 4088 conditioned zones traced; component-level precision 0.993 and recall 1.000 against the declared structure (72862 of 72867 declared zone-component pairs found, 538 found but not declared); 3898 of 4088 zones exact.** 34 files are out of scope (below).

*Traced*: a non-empty serving chain was recovered for a conditioned zone (a zone with `ZoneHVAC:EquipmentConnections`). *Precision / recall / exact*: as in `docs/topology_survey.md` (same reference `declared_chain`, built only from the objects that declare the system structure). System types are derived from object types, never from names.

### Mismatches by object type (zone-component pairs)

Of the 538 traced-but-not-declared pairs, 436 are parts that a declared object names in a `<x>_name` field without an object-type field (for example the fan and mixer of a fan-powered terminal, or the heat exchanger and fans of an energy recovery ventilator); the reference does not follow such references, the tracer reaches them through their nodes.

| FAN | 0 | 275 |
| HEATEXCHANGER | 0 | 107 |
| AIRLOOPHVAC | 0 | 48 |
| COIL | 0 | 34 |
| PIPE | 0 | 31 |
| PUMP | 0 | 21 |
| SOLARCOLLECTOR | 5 | 5 |
| WATERHEATER | 0 | 6 |
| CHILLER | 0 | 5 |
| BOILER | 0 | 5 |
| WATERUSE | 0 | 1 |

Conditioned zones without a traced chain: 0

### What was fixed, and what the remaining mismatches are

Tracer and loader bugs found by this survey and fixed (2026-11-19), none by name: (1) object types written in fields are case-insensitive in EnergyPlus (`AirloopHVAC:UnitarySystem` in a Branch); `EPModel.objs/obj` and the tracer now match them so, and index names (a 130-zone model took minutes in the reference walk); (2) zone equipment without an air inlet node (baseboards, radiant and cooling panels, high-temperature radiant heaters, zone exhaust fans) is linked to its zone through the zone's equipment list; (3) node fields whose names do not say inlet/outlet get their direction from the field description (ideal loads, ventilated slab, unit ventilator, hybrid unitary, zone evaporative cooler, outdoor-air unit, heat pump water heater, the bypass duct of a changeover-bypass VAV unit); (4) declared equipment that names no node of its own (energy recovery ventilator, heat-exchanger-assisted coil systems, desiccant dehumidifier, electric baseboards) becomes a component carrying its branch's and its parts' ports; (5) the rest of every air loop the trace passes (relief fan, exhaust side of a run-around or heat-recovery loop) and the parts a chain component declares (a heat pump water heater's tank and fan) are added and traced upstream in turn; (6) `<x>_object_type` pairs spelt `<x>_object_name` (VRF terminals) or `<y>_<x>_name` (window air conditioner) are read; (7) performance-data and airflow-network objects (`CoilPerformance:*`, `UnitarySystemPerformance:*`, `AirflowNetwork:*`, ...) are not components. The reference was corrected in the same ways where it read declarations too narrowly (case of object types, NodeList splitter outlets, the pair spellings, a zone outdoor-air unit's equipment list, and the plant loop of every demand-side component, not only of those with 'water' in a field name).

Remaining differences, not tracer errors: parts that a declared object names without an object-type field (the fan and mixer of fan-powered terminals, the heat exchanger and fans of energy recovery ventilators, the fans of outdoor-air units) are found by the tracer and not by the reference; the reference does not follow `AirLoopHVAC:DedicatedOutdoorAirSystem`, which feeds several air loops through `AirLoopHVAC:Mixer/Splitter`; the tracer includes what is upstream of a supply-side component (the service-water pump ahead of a heat pump water heater's tank, the return plenum an ideal-loads system draws from); `SolarCollector:UnglazedTranspired:Multisystem` names its collector, not the reverse.

## Stage 2: scenes (auto mode, with simulation and rendering)

**719 example files; 677 scenes built and 677 rendered: 677 of 685 in scope, 677 of 677 that EnergyPlus 22.2 runs as shipped.** Each scene: `python -m thermoscene auto <model> --render` (weather file nearest to Site:Location, run periods 14-15 Jan and 15-16 Jul, default zone = conditioned zone with the longest traced chain, automatic cameras), one preview frame per camera. A simulation failure is checked by running the unmodified example file (design days, same weather file); if that fails too, the failure is EnergyPlus's own. Scene build after the simulation: median 12 s, max 549 s; whole run per model (simulation, build, rendering): median 23 s, total 9.6 h of worker time.

Totals over built scenes: 10409 surfaces, 25096 generated elements, 9421 simulated components; 178 scenes with a plenum, 598 with generated HVAC elements. Cameras: ZoneCamera 677, RooftopCamera 367, PlenumCamera 177. Element classes: Diffuser 10576, ReturnGrille 5228, Duct 4838, EquipmentCasing 1861, Pipe 1242, CondenserFan 332, Louver 281, Valve 187, TerminalBox 177, CondenserCoil 166, CoilSection 151, Motor 57.

### Failure taxonomy

| Stage | Reason | Files | Examples |
|---|---|---|---|
| out of scope | no zones (plant, component or weather test file) | 30 | ConstSpeedBranchPumpExercise.idf, ExteriorLightsAndEq.idf, GHTIn.idf, Minimal.idf, PipingSystem_Underground_TwoPipe.idf ... |
| EnergyPlus (model as shipped fails) | **  Fatal  ** GetSurfaceData: Errors discovered, program terminates. | 4 | 5ZoneAirCooledWithSlab.idf, LgOffVAVusingBasement.idf, SingleFamilyHouse_HP_Slab.idf, SingleFamilyHouse_HP_Slab_Dehumidification.idf |
| out of scope | Parametric:* objects (must be run through ParametricPreprocessor first) | 3 | 1ZoneParameterAspect.idf, LBuildingAppGRotPar.idf, ParametricInsulation-5ZoneAirCooled.idf |
| EnergyPlus (model as shipped fails) | **  Fatal  ** Btwxt: Errors discovered, program terminates. | 1 | 1ZoneDataCenterCRAC_wApproachTemp.idf |
| EnergyPlus (model as shipped fails) | **  Fatal  ** Preceding errors cause termination | 1 | PythonPluginTestMathAndKill.idf |
| EnergyPlus (model as shipped fails) | **  Fatal  ** Python Plugin "PythonPluginCustomSchedule.CoolingSetPoint" returned 1 to indicate EnergyPlus should abort | 1 | PythonPluginCustomSchedule.idf |
| EnergyPlus (model as shipped fails) | Severe  ** Table:Lookup "HPACCOOLPLFFPLR_TABLE": Target and Gridded Data do not have the same dimensions. | 1 | EMSTestMathAndKill.idf |
| out of scope | written for EnergyPlus 7.2 (needs IDFVersionUpdater to 22.2.0) | 1 | RefBldgSmallOfficeNew2004_v1.4_7.2_2B_USA_AZ_PHOENIX_V720.idf |

Failures by stage: out of scope 34, EnergyPlus (model as shipped fails) 8.

Notes. Pipeline bugs found by this stage and fixed (2026-11-19): simplified rectangular surfaces (`Wall:Exterior`, `Roof`, `Floor:Adiabatic`, `*:Interzone`, ...) and the interzone sub-surfaces were not read; EnergyPlus overrides the requested time step for Conduction Finite Difference models (20 per hour) and the time axis came out empty; a model with one time step per hour reports HVAC sub-steps only (auto mode now uses at least 4 per hour); scene paths longer than 260 characters made the USD write fail on Windows; an incomplete simulation cache (results without `eplusout.err`) was re-used; plenums across an interzone ceiling whose boundary names a zone were not found. The 8 'model as shipped fails' files also fail when the unmodified example file is run by EnergyPlus 22.2 (four need the Slab/Basement ground preprocessors that EP-Launch runs to create their other-side coefficients, two have inconsistent lookup tables, one has node-connection errors and one a Python plugin that aborts the run; two of them are EMS/plugin 'kill' tests). DElight models write scratch files into the model folder (EnergyPlus runs there so relative file names resolve), so two of them collided when run in parallel, and two supermarket models ran out of memory with six parallel workers; all four were re-run one at a time and built. Five models whose zone has no Floor or Ceiling surface of its own (a ground floor over a Kiva or basement foundation, an underground pipe test) were built without a camera, because the indoor candidate grid of `render/cameras.auto_pose` was the plan of the space's floors and ceilings; it now falls back to the plan of its roofs, then to the outline of all its surfaces, and the five were re-run and rendered.
